**Research-first (RESEARCH §1160, same-day; chain visible → build).** 15-min dive (WebSearch, dates verified). **Long-form coherence = a TWO-TIMESCALE engine.** *"Fusing Memory and Attention" (arXiv:2603.21282, ESWA 2026)* — LSTM/recurrent memory holds LOCAL continuity but loses long-range structure; attention holds GLOBAL structure but not moment-to-moment feel → the fix is a hybrid that runs BOTH. Paired with *"Efficient Long-Sequence Diffusion" (arXiv:2603.00576, 2026)* — a structured state-space model carries long-range context while a refinement pass fixes local detail (same slow-scaffold / fast-detail split). Lineage: **Brian Eno *Reflection* (2017)**.
